It is clear that only the first line in the r.h.s. can give the non-negligible contribution to
W_{\mu\nu}
. Indeed,
matrix element
\langle\bar{\psi}(x)\big[A_{\nu_{\perp}}(x)\sigma_{\ast j}-A_{j}(x)\sigma_{\ast\nu_{\perp}}\big]\psi(0)\rangle_{A}

vanishes for unpolarized hadrons due to parity, see Eq. (
60
). In the third line in r.h.s., neither matrix element can produce
s

so the corresponding contribution is again
\sim{p_{1\mu}q^{\perp}_{\nu}m_{\perp}^{2}\over\alpha_{q}s^{2}}
while contribution from the last line
is even smaller, of order of
{p_{1\mu}q^{\perp}_{\nu}m_{\perp}^{4}\over\alpha_{q}s^{3}}
. Thus, we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{2p_{1\mu}\over s}p_{2}^{\mu^{\prime}}{N_{c}\over s}\langle A,B|[\bar{\psi}_{B}(0)\gamma_{\mu^{\prime}}\psi_{A}(0)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{A}(x)\gamma_{\nu_{\perp}}\Xi_{2}(x)\big]+\mu^{\prime}\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle

(79)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{p_{1\mu}\over s^{3}}\Big\{\langle\bar{\psi}{\not\!\!A}(x){\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{\nu_{\perp}}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}(0){\not\!p}_{1}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+i\langle\bar{\psi}{\not\!\!A}(x){\not\!p}_{2}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

It remains to prove that the last term in Eq. (
76
) proportional to
p_{2\mu}
is small. One can rewrite that term
similarly to Eq. (
65
) with replacement

p_{1}\leftrightarrow p_{2}
and (projectile matrix elements)
\leftrightarrow

(target ones). After that, the proof repeats arguments after Eq. (
65
) and one obtains the estimate

{2p_{2\mu}\over s^{2}}N_{c}\langle A,B\big[\bar{\psi}_{A}(x)\gamma_{\nu_{\perp}}\Xi_{2}(x)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}(0){\not\!p}_{1}\psi_{A}(0)\big]|A,B\rangle~\sim~p_{2\mu}q^{\perp}_{\nu}{m_{\perp}^{2}\over\beta_{q}s^{2}}

(80)

Similarly, by repeating arguments from Section
6.1.1
with replacement
p_{1}\leftrightarrow p_{2}
and projectile matrix
elements
\leftrightarrow
target ones, one can demonstrate that terms in Eq. (
75
) with
\mu,\nu
both longitudinal or both transverse
are small in comparison to terms listed in Eq. (
39
).

Thus,

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{W}^{(1)}_{3\mu\nu}(x)~=~{N_{c}\over s}\langle A,B|[\bar{\psi}_{A}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Xi_{2}(x)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{A}(0)\big]+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69054pt=~{p_{1\mu}\over s^{2}}\big[\langle\bar{\psi}{\not\!\!A}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp}){\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{\nu_{\perp}}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}(0){\not\!p}_{1}{1\over\beta}\psi(x_{\ast},x^{\prime}_{\perp})\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+~i\langle\bar{\psi}{\not\!\!A}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp}){\not\!p}_{2}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}{1\over\beta}\psi(x_{\ast},x^{\prime}_{\perp})\rangle_{B}~+~\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\Big]~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(81)

Using QCD equation of motion and formulas from Appendix, we obtain the corresponding contribution to
W_{\mu\nu}
in the form

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 16\pi^{4}}{1\over s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\ast}d^{2}x_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}-i\beta x_{\ast}+i(q,x)_{\perp}}

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt\times~\langle A,B|\big[\bar{\psi}_{A}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Xi_{2}(x)\big][\bar{\psi}_{B}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{A}(0)\big]|A,B\rangle+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{1\over 64\pi^{6}N_{c}}{p_{1\mu}\over s^{3}}\!\int\!dx_{\ast}dx_{\bullet}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}d^{2}x_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\!\int\!dx_{\ast}d^{2}x^{\prime}_{\perp}e^{-i\beta x_{\bullet}+i(q-k,x^{\prime})_{\perp}}

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt\times~\big[\langle\bar{\psi}{\not\!\!A}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp}){\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{\nu_{\perp}}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}(0){\not\!p}_{1}{1\over\beta}\psi(x_{\ast},x^{\prime}_{\perp})\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt+~i\langle\bar{\psi}{\not\!\!A}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp}){\not\!p}_{2}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}{1\over\beta}\psi(x_{\ast},x^{\prime}_{\perp})\rangle_{B}~+~\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{p_{1\mu}\over\beta_{q}sN_{c}}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}\Big[k_{\nu}f_{1}^{f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp}){\bar{f}}_{\perp}^{f}(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt-~(q-k)_{\nu}{k_{\perp}^{2}\over m^{2}}h_{1f}^{\perp}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp}){\bar{h}}_{\perp}^{f}(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})\Big]~+~\mu\leftrightarrow\nu

(82)

Same as in previous Section, the term with
x\leftrightarrow 0
exchange
leads to Eq. (
82
) with
f_{1}\leftrightarrow{\bar{f}}_{1}
and
h_{1}^{\perp}\leftrightarrow{\bar{h}}_{1}^{\perp}
replacement so we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptW^{(1)}_{3\mu\nu}~=~{p_{1\mu}\over\beta_{q}sN_{c}}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}\Big[k_{\nu}F^{f}(q,k_{\perp})-~(q-k)_{\nu}{k_{\perp}^{2}\over m^{2}}H^{f}(q,k_{\perp})\Big]~+~\mu\leftrightarrow\nu

(83)

Repeating arguments from previous Section, it is possible to show that the
contribution of the fourth term

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{W}^{(1)}_{4\mu\nu}(x)~=~{N_{c}\over s}\langle A,B|[\bar{\psi}_{A}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Xi_{2}(x)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{A}(0)\big]+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69054pt=~{p_{1\mu}\over s^{2}}\big[\langle\bar{\psi}\gamma_{\nu_{\perp}}{\not\!p}_{2}{\not\!\!A}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0){\not\!p}_{1}\psi(x_{\ast},x^{\prime}_{\perp})\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt-~i\langle\bar{\psi}(x_{\ast},x^{\prime}_{\perp}{\not\!p}_{2}{\not\!\!A}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}\psi(x_{\ast},x^{\prime}_{\perp})\rangle_{B}~+~\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\Big]~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(84)

doubles that of the third term so we get the full contribution of the terms with one quark-antiquark-gluon operators
in the form

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptW^{(1)}_{\mu\nu}~=~{2\over N_{c}}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}\Big[\Big({p_{1\mu}k^{\perp}_{\nu}\over\beta_{q}s}+{p_{2\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}\over\alpha_{q}s}\Big)F^{f}(q,k_{\perp})

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~\Big({p_{1\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}\over\beta_{q}s}{k_{\perp}^{2}\over m^{2}}+{p_{2\mu}k_{\perp}^{\nu}\over\alpha_{q}s}{(q-k)_{\perp}^{2}\over m^{2}}\Big)H^{f}(q,k_{\perp})\Big]~+~\mu\leftrightarrow\nu

(85)

This result agrees with the corresponding
1/Q
terms in Ref.
[
20
]
.

6.2
Term with two quark-quark-gluon operators coming from
\Xi_{1}
and
\Xi_{2}

Let us start with the first term in the r.h.s. of Eq. (
42
).
Performing Fierz transformation (
237
) we obtain

\hskip 2.84526pt{N_{c}\over s}\langle A,B|({\bar{\psi}}_{A}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x))({\bar{\psi}}_{B}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Xi_{1}^{n}(0))+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle+x\leftrightarrow 0~=~g_{\mu\nu}\check{V}_{1}+\check{V}_{2\mu\nu}+\check{V}_{3\mu\nu}

(86)

where

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt\check{V}_{1}~=~{N_{c}\over 2s}\langle A,B|-[{\bar{\psi}}_{A}^{n}(x)\Xi_{1}^{m}(0)][{\bar{\psi}}_{B}^{n}(0)\Xi_{2}^{m}(x)]+[{\bar{\psi}}_{A}^{m}(x)\gamma_{5}\Xi_{1}^{n}(0)][{\bar{\psi}}_{B}^{n}(0)\gamma_{5}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x)]

(87)

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt+~[{\bar{\psi}}_{A}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\alpha}\Xi_{1}^{m}(0)][{\bar{\psi}}_{B}^{n}(0)\gamma^{\alpha}\Xi_{2}^{n}(x)]+[{\bar{\psi}}_{A}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\alpha}\gamma_{5}\Xi_{1}^{m}(0)][{\bar{\psi}}_{B}^{n}(0)\gamma^{\alpha}\gamma_{5}\Xi_{2}^{n}(x)]|A,B\rangle+x\leftrightarrow 0,

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{V}_{2\mu\nu}~=~{N_{c}\over 2s}\langle A,B|-~[{\bar{\psi}}_{A}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Xi_{1}^{n}(0)][{\bar{\psi}}_{B}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x)]

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt-~[{\bar{\psi}}_{A}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\Xi_{1}^{n}(0)][{\bar{\psi}}_{B}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x)]+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle+x\leftrightarrow 0,

(88)

and

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{V}_{3\mu\nu}~=~{N_{c}\over 2s}\langle A,B|[({\bar{\psi}}_{A}^{m}(x)\sigma_{\mu\alpha}\Xi_{1}^{n}(0)][{\bar{\psi}}_{B}^{n}(0)\sigma_{\nu}^{~\alpha}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x)]+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt-~g_{\mu\nu}[{\bar{\psi}}_{A}^{m}(x)\sigma^{\alpha\beta}\Xi_{1}^{n}(0)][{\bar{\psi}}_{B}^{n}(0)\sigma_{\alpha\beta}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x)|A,B\rangle+x\leftrightarrow 0

(89)

It is convenient to define
\check{V}_{3\mu\nu}
to be traceless. In next Sections, we will consider these terms in turn.

6.2.1
Term propotional to
g_{\mu\nu}

Using
\Xi_{1}~=~-{g\not{p}_{2}\over s}\gamma^{i}B_{i}{1\over\alpha+i\epsilon}\psi_{A}
and

\Xi_{2}~=~-{g\not{p}_{1}\over s}\gamma^{i}A_{i}{1\over\beta+i\epsilon}\psi_{B}
from Eq. (
26
) and
extracting color-singlet contributions one obtains

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{V}_{1}~=~{1\over 2s^{3}}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\times~\Big\{-\Big[\langle{\bar{\psi}}A_{i}(x){\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B_{j}(0){\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}-\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\Big[\langle{\bar{\psi}}A_{i}(x)\gamma_{k}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B_{j}(0)\gamma^{k}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 39.83385pt+~{2\over s}\Big[\langle{\bar{\psi}}A_{i}(x){\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B_{j}(0){\not\!p}_{2}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt+~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\Big]\Big\}~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(90)

Let us start with the first term. Using Eq. (
257
) and the fact that
\langle\bar{\psi}(x)\big[A_{k}\sigma_{\ast j}-A_{j}(x)\sigma_{\ast k}\big]\psi(0)\rangle_{A}=0
(see the footnote
11
),
we obtain

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-{1\over 2s^{3}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}{{\not\!\!A}}(x){\not\!p}_{2}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}{{\not\!\!B}}(0){\not\!p}_{1}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}-{1\over 4s^{2}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}{{\not\!\!A}}(x)\gamma_{i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}{{\not\!\!B}}(0)\gamma^{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt-~{1\over 8s^{2}}[\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{i}(x)\sigma_{jk}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B_{i}(0)\sigma^{jk}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt=~-{1\over 2s^{3}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}{{\not\!\!A}}(x){\not\!p}_{2}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}{{\not\!\!B}}(0){\not\!p}_{1}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}\Big[1+O\big({q_{\perp}^{2}\over s}\big)\Big]

(91)

where we used the fact that projectile and target matrix elements in the two last terms in the l.h.s. cannot produce factor of
s
.

Next, consider second term in Eq. (
90
). Using Eqs. (
253
) and (
257
), one can rewrite is as

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 2s^{3}}\Big[\langle{\bar{\psi}}A_{i}(x)\gamma_{k}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B_{j}(0)\gamma^{k}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 39.83385pt=~{1\over s^{3}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}{{\not\!\!A}}(x){\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}{{\not\!\!B}}(0){\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

(92)

Similarly, from Eq. (
257
) we get the third term in the form

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over s^{4}}\Big[\langle{\bar{\psi}}A_{i}(x){\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B_{j}(0){\not\!p}_{2}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{1\over 4s^{2}}\Big[\langle{\bar{\psi}}{\breve{A}}_{i}(x)\gamma^{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}{\breve{B}}_{j}(0)\gamma^{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\Big]

(93)

Since both projectile and target matrix elements cannot give factor
s
this contribution is
O\big({q_{\perp}^{2}\over s}\big)
in comparison to that of
the two first terms.
Thus, we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{V}_{1}~=~{1\over s^{3}}\Big({1\over 2}\langle{\bar{\psi}}{{\not\!\!A}}(x){\not\!p}_{2}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}{{\not\!\!B}}(0){\not\!p}_{1}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+~\langle{\bar{\psi}}{{\not\!\!A}}(x){\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}{{\not\!\!B}}(0){\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}\Big)\Big[1+O\big({q_{\perp}^{2}\over s}\big)\Big]~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(94)

Next, using QCD equations of motion (
276
), (
279
) and formulas from Appendix
11.2
, we obtain the contribution to
W_{\mu\nu}
in the form

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptg_{\mu\nu}V_{1}(q)~=~{g_{\mu\nu}\over 16\pi^{4}N_{c}}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\ast}d^{2}x_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}-i\beta_{q}x_{\ast}+i(q,x)_{\perp}}\check{V}_{1}(x)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=~{g_{\mu\nu}\over\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}sN_{c}}\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}\Big[(k,q-k)_{\perp}F^{f}(q,k_{\perp})-~{1\over 2m^{2}}k_{\perp}^{2}(q-k)_{\perp}^{2}H^{f}(q,k_{\perp})\Big]

(95)

where replacements
f_{1}^{f}\leftrightarrow{\bar{f}}^{f}_{1}
and
h_{1f}^{\perp}\leftrightarrow{\bar{h}}_{1f}^{\perp}
come from
x\leftrightarrow 0
term.

6.2.2
Term with TMD’s
f_{1}

Separating color-singlet contributions one can rewrite Eq. (
88
) as

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{V}_{2\mu\nu}~=~-{1\over 2s^{3}}\big\{\langle{\bar{\psi}}A_{i}(x)\gamma_{\mu}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B_{j}(0)\gamma_{\nu}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\}~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(96)

We need to consider three cases: both
\mu
and
\nu
are transverse, both of them are longitudinal, and

\mu
is longitudinal and
\nu
transverse (plus
vice versa
).

In the first case we can use formula (
254
) and get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{V}_{2\mu_{\perp}\nu_{\perp}}~=~-{1\over 2s^{3}}\big\{\langle{\bar{\psi}}A_{i}(x)\gamma_{\mu_{\perp}}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B_{j}(0)\gamma_{\nu_{\perp}}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\}~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=~-{g_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}\over s^{3}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}{\not\!\!A}(x){\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}{\not\!\!B}(0){\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(97)

which gives the contribution to
W_{\mu\nu}
in the form

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptV_{2\mu_{\perp}\nu_{\perp}}~=~{1\over 16\pi^{4}N_{c}}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\ast}d^{2}x_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}-i\beta_{q}x_{\ast}+i(q,x)_{\perp}}\check{V}_{2\mu_{\perp}\nu_{\perp}}(x)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-{g_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}\over\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}sN_{c}}\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}(k,q-k)_{\perp}F^{f}(q,k_{\perp})

(98)

where we again used formulas from Appendices
11.2
and
11.3
.

Next, if both
\mu
and
\nu
are longitudinal, we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{V}_{2\mu\nu}~=~-{2\over s^{5}}(p_{1\mu}p_{2\nu}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu)\big\{\langle{\bar{\psi}}A_{i}(x){\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B_{j}(0){\not\!p}_{2}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\}~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(99)

Using formula (
256
) we rewrite r.h.s. of Eq. (
96
) as follows

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{V}_{2\mu\nu}~=~-{1\over 2s^{3}}(p_{1\mu}p_{2\nu}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu)\big\{\langle{\bar{\psi}}\breve{A}_{i}(x)\gamma^{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}\breve{B}_{j}(0)\gamma^{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\}~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(100)

Since matrix elements in the r.h.s. cannot give factor
s
, the contribution of this term to
W_{\mu\nu}
is
\sim{q_{\perp}^{2}\over s}
times that
of Eq. (
98
).

Finally, let us consider the case when one index is longitudinal and the other transverse. Using Eq. (
259
) we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{V}_{2\mu\nu}^{\parallel\perp}~=~-{1\over s^{4}}\big\{p_{2\mu}\langle{\bar{\psi}}A_{i}(x){\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B_{j}(0)\gamma_{\nu_{\perp}}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+~p_{1\mu}\langle{\bar{\psi}}A_{i}(x)\gamma_{\nu_{\perp}}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B_{j}(0){\not\!p}_{2}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\}~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-{1\over 2s^{3}}\big\{p_{2\mu}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x)\gamma^{i}A_{\nu}(x){1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}{{\not\!\!B}}(0){\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+~p_{1\mu}\langle{\bar{\psi}}{{\not\!\!A}}(x){\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)\gamma^{i}B_{\nu}(0){1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\}~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(101)

It is clear that
\langle{\bar{\psi}}{{\not\!\!A}}(x){\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}
and

\langle{\bar{\psi}}{{\not\!\!B}}(0){\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}
bring one factor
s
so

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{V}_{2\mu\nu}^{\parallel\perp}~\sim~{p_{1\mu}q_{\perp_{\nu}}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\over\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}s^{2}}m_{\perp}^{2}~~{\rm or}~~\sim~{p_{2\mu}q_{\perp_{\nu}}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\over\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}s^{2}}m_{\perp}^{2}

(102)

which is
{1\over\alpha_{q}s}
or
{1\over\beta_{q}s}
correction in comparison to Eq. (
85
). Thus, the contribution to
W_{\mu\nu}
is given by Eq. (
98
)

\hskip 0.0ptV_{2\mu\nu}~=~-{g_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}\over\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}sN_{c}}\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}(k,q-k)_{\perp}F^{f}(q,k_{\perp})

(103)

6.2.3
Term with TMD’s
h_{1}^{\perp}

Let us consider now

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{V}^{\prime}_{3\mu\nu}~=~{N_{c}\over 2s}\langle A,B|[({\bar{\psi}}_{A}^{m}(x)\sigma_{\mu\alpha}\Xi_{1}^{m}(0)][{\bar{\psi}}_{B}^{n}(0)\sigma_{\nu}^{~\alpha}\Xi_{2}^{n}(x)]|A,B\rangle+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu+x\leftrightarrow 0

(104)

(the trace will be subtracted after the calculation). Separating color-singlet contributions, we get

\hskip 0.0pt\check{V}^{\prime}_{3\mu\nu}~=~{1\over 2s^{3}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}A_{i}(x)\sigma_{\mu\alpha}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B_{j}(0)\sigma_{\nu}^{~\alpha}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu+x\leftrightarrow 0

(105)

First case is when
\mu
and
\nu
are transverse

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{V}^{\prime}_{3\mu_{\perp}\nu_{\perp}}~=~-{1\over 2s^{3}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{i}(x)\sigma_{\mu_{\perp}k}\sigma_{\ast j}{1\over\alpha}\psi)0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{j}(0)\sigma_{\nu_{\perp}}^{~k}\sigma_{{\bullet}i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~{1\over s^{4}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{i}(x)\sigma_{{\bullet}\mu_{\perp}}\sigma_{\ast j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{j}(0)\sigma_{\ast\nu_{\perp}}\sigma_{{\bullet}i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu+x\leftrightarrow 0

(106)

With the help of Eq. (
240
) the first term in the r.h.s. turns to

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt{1\over 2s^{3}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{i}(x)[g_{\mu j}\sigma_{\ast k}-g_{jk}\sigma_{\ast\mu_{\perp}}]{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{j}(0)[g_{\nu i}\sigma_{\bullet}^{~k}-\delta_{i}^{k}\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}]{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu+x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt=~{1\over 2s^{3}}\Big(\langle{\bar{\psi}}A_{\nu}(x)\sigma_{\ast k}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B_{\mu}(0)\sigma_{\bullet}^{~k}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt-~\langle{\bar{\psi}}A_{\nu}(x)\sigma_{\ast\mu_{\perp}}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{j}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}-~\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{i}(x)\sigma_{\ast i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B_{\mu}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt+~\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{i}(x)\sigma_{\ast\mu_{\perp}}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B_{i}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}\Big)+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu+x\leftrightarrow 0

(107)

After some algebra, it can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-{g_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}\over 2s^{3}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{i}(x)\sigma_{\ast i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{j}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt+~{1\over s^{3}}\big\{\langle{\bar{\psi}}\big(A_{k}\sigma_{\ast\mu_{\perp}}-{1\over 2}g_{\mu k}\sigma_{\ast j}A^{j}\big)(x){1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt\times~\langle{\bar{\psi}}\big(B^{k}\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}-{1\over 2}\delta_{\nu}^{k}\sigma_{{\bullet}j}B^{j}\big)(0){1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\big\}+x\leftrightarrow 0

(108)

where again we used property (
60
). Using QCD equations of motion (
277
), (
279
) and parametrization (
283
)
one can write the corresponding contribution to
W_{\mu\nu}
as

\displaystyle\hskip-31.29802ptV^{\prime}_{3\mu_{\perp}\nu_{\perp}}~=~{g^{\perp}_{\mu\nu}\over 2\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}sN_{c}}\!\int d^{2}k_{\perp}{k_{\perp}^{2}(q-k)_{\perp}^{2}\over m^{2}}H^{f}(q,k_{\perp})

(109)

\displaystyle\hskip 8.53581pt+~{1\over\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}sN_{c}}\!\int{d^{2}k_{\perp}\over m^{2}}\big[(k^{\perp}_{\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu)(k,q-k)_{\perp}-k_{\perp}^{2}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.53581pt-~(q-k_{\perp})^{2}k^{\perp}_{\mu}k^{\perp}_{\nu}-{g_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}\over 2}k_{\perp}^{2}(q-k_{\perp})^{2}\big]H_{A}^{f}(q,k_{\perp})

where we introduced the notation

\displaystyle\hskip-31.29802ptH_{A}^{f}(q,k_{\perp})~\equiv~h_{A}^{f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp}){\bar{h}}_{A}^{f}(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})+h_{A}^{f}\leftrightarrow{\bar{h}}_{A}^{f}\

(110)

The second term in Eq. (
106
) can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{V}^{\prime}_{3\mu_{\perp}\nu_{\perp}}~=~-{1\over 4s^{2}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{i}(x)\big(g^{\perp}_{\mu j}-i\epsilon_{\mu_{\perp}j}\gamma_{5}-i\sigma_{\mu_{\perp}j}-{2i\over s}g^{\perp}_{\mu j}\sigma_{{\bullet}\ast}\big){1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\times~\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{j}(0)\big(g^{\perp}_{i\nu}-i\epsilon_{i\nu_{\perp}}\gamma_{5}+i\sigma_{i\nu_{\perp}}+{2\over s}g^{\perp}_{i\nu}\sigma_{\ast{\bullet}}\big){1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu+x\leftrightarrow 0

(111)

where we used Eq. (
240
).
It is clear that neither projectile no target matrix element in the r.h.s. can bring factor
s
so

\hskip 2.84526pt\check{V}^{\prime}_{3\mu_{\perp}\nu_{\perp}}~\sim~{m_{\perp}^{4}\over\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}s^{2}}

(112)

which is
O\big({m_{\perp}^{2}\over s}\big)
in comparison to Eq. (
109
).

Next, consider the case when both
\mu
and
\nu
are longitudinal. The non-vanishing terms are

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{V}^{\prime}_{3\mu\nu}~=~{4p_{1\mu}p_{2\nu}\over s^{2}}{1\over 2s^{3}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}A_{i}(x)\sigma_{\ast\alpha}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B_{j}(0)\sigma_{\bullet}^{~\alpha}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~{4p_{1\nu}p_{2\mu}\over s^{2}}{1\over 2s^{3}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}A_{i}(x)\sigma_{{\bullet}\alpha}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B_{j}(0)\sigma_{\ast}^{~\alpha}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

(113)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~{4p_{2\mu}p_{1\nu}\over s^{2}}{1\over 2s^{3}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{i}(x)\sigma_{{\bullet}k}\sigma_{\ast j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{j}(0)\sigma_{\ast}^{~k}\sigma_{{\bullet}i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu+x\leftrightarrow 0

The first two terms in the r.h.s. can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{V}^{\prime}_{3\mu\nu}~=~{g^{\parallel}_{\mu\nu}\over s^{3}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{i}(x)\sigma_{\ast j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{j}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{g^{\parallel}_{\mu\nu}\over 2s^{3}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{i}(x)\sigma_{\ast i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{j}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~{g^{\parallel}_{\mu\nu}\over s^{3}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}\big(A^{i}(x)\sigma_{\ast j}-{g_{ij}\over 2}A^{k}(x)\sigma_{\ast k}\big){1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{j}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+x\leftrightarrow 0

(114)

The corresponding contribution to
W_{\mu\nu}
has the form

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptV^{\prime}_{3\mu_{\parallel}\nu_{\parallel}}~=~-{g^{\parallel}_{\mu\nu}\over 2\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}sN_{c}}\!\int d^{2}k_{\perp}{k_{\perp}^{2}(q-k)_{\perp}^{2}\over m^{2}}H^{f}(q,k_{\perp})

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~{g^{\parallel}_{\mu\nu}\over\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}sN_{c}}\!\int{d^{2}k_{\perp}\over m^{2}}\big[(k,q-k)_{\perp}^{2}-{1\over 2}k_{\perp}^{2}(q-k)_{\perp}^{2}\big]H_{A}^{f}(q,k_{\perp})

(115)

where again we used QCD equations of motion (
277
), (
279
) and parametrization (
283
).

Next, it is easy to see that the third term in Eq. (
113
) is small in comparison to Eq. (
115
):

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~{4p_{2\mu}p_{1\nu}\over s^{2}}{1\over 2s^{3}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{i}(x)\sigma_{{\bullet}k}\sigma_{\ast j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{j}(0)\sigma_{\ast}^{~k}\sigma_{{\bullet}i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu+x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-~{p_{2\mu}p_{1\nu}\over 2s^{3}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{i}(x)\big[g_{jk}+i\epsilon_{jk}\gamma_{5}+i\sigma_{jk}-{2i\over s}g_{jk}\sigma_{{\bullet}\ast}\big]{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\times~\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{j}(0)\big[g_{ik}-i\epsilon_{ik}\gamma_{5}+i\sigma_{ik}+{2\over s}g_{ik}\sigma_{\ast{\bullet}}\big]{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}~\sim~{q_{\perp}^{2}\over s}\times{g^{\parallel}_{\mu\nu}\over\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}s}

(116)

because neither projectile no target matrix element can bring factor
s
.

Finally, take one of the indices (say,
\mu
) longitudinal and the other transverse. From Eq. (
105
) we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{V}_{3\mu_{\parallel}\nu_{\perp}}~=~-{p_{1\mu}\over s^{4}}\langle[{\bar{\psi}}A^{i}(x)\sigma_{{\bullet}k}\sigma_{\ast j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{j}(0)\sigma_{\nu_{\perp}}^{~k}\sigma_{{\bullet}i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~{ip_{1\mu}\over 2s^{3}}\langle[{\bar{\psi}}A^{i}(x)\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}\sigma_{\ast j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{j}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu+x\leftrightarrow 0

(117)

Using formulas (
240
) this can be rewritten as follows

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{V}_{3\mu_{\parallel}\nu_{\perp}}~=~{ip_{1\mu}\over 2s^{3}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{i}(x)\big[g_{jk}+i\epsilon_{jk}\gamma_{5}+i\sigma_{jk}-{2i\over s}g_{jk}\sigma_{{\bullet}\ast}\big]{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt\times~\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{j}(0)\big[g_{i\nu_{\perp}}\sigma_{{\bullet}k}-g_{ik}\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}\big]{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~{ip_{1\mu}\over 2s^{3}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{i}(x)\big[g_{j\nu_{\perp}}+i\epsilon_{j\nu_{\perp}}\gamma_{5}+i\sigma_{j\nu_{\perp}}-{2i\over s}g_{j\nu_{\perp}}\sigma_{{\bullet}\ast}\big]{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt\times~\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{j}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu+x\leftrightarrow 0

(118)

As we discussed above, projectile matrix elements in the r.h.s. like
\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{j}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

can bring factor
s
but the target matrix elements cannot so the corresponding contribution to
W_{\mu\nu}
is of order

\hskip 2.84526pt\check{V}_{3\mu_{\parallel}\nu_{\perp}}~\sim~\big(p_{1\mu}q^{\perp}_{\nu}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\big){m_{\perp}^{2}\over\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}s^{2}}

(119)

which is
O\big({q_{\perp}^{2}\over s}\big)
in comparison to Eq. (
85
).

Next, the sum of Eqs. (
109
) and (
115
) is

\displaystyle\hskip-31.29802ptV^{\prime}_{3\mu\nu}~=~{g^{\perp}_{\mu\nu}-g^{\parallel}_{\mu\nu}\over 2\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}sN_{c}}\!\int d^{2}k_{\perp}{k_{\perp}^{2}(q-k)_{\perp}^{2}\over m^{2}}H^{f}(q,k_{\perp})

(120)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~{1\over\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}sN_{c}}\!\int d^{2}k_{\perp}{1\over m^{2}}\big\{[k^{\perp}_{\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu](k,q-k)_{\perp}-k_{\perp}^{2}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~(q-k_{\perp})^{2}k^{\perp}_{\mu}k^{\perp}_{\nu}-{g_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}\over 2}k_{\perp}^{2}(q-k_{\perp})^{2}-g^{\parallel}_{\mu\nu}\big[(k,q-k)_{\perp}^{2}-{1\over 2}k_{\perp}^{2}(q-k)_{\perp}^{2}\big]\big\}H_{A}^{f}(q,k_{\perp})

so subtracting trace we obtain

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptV_{3\mu\nu}~=~V^{\prime}_{3\mu\nu}-g_{\mu\nu}{V^{\prime}_{3}}_{\xi}^{~\xi}

(121)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{g^{\perp}_{\mu\nu}-g^{\parallel}_{\mu\nu}\over 2\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}sN_{c}}\!\int d^{2}k_{\perp}{1\over m^{2}}k_{\perp}^{2}(q-k)_{\perp}^{2}H^{f}(q,k_{\perp})

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~{1\over\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}sN_{c}}\!\int d^{2}k_{\perp}{1\over m^{2}}\big\{[k^{\perp}_{\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu](k,q-k)_{\perp}-k_{\perp}^{2}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~(q-k_{\perp})^{2}k^{\perp}_{\mu}k^{\perp}_{\nu}+g_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}(k,q-k)_{\perp}^{2}-g_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}k_{\perp}^{2}(q-k_{\perp})^{2}\big]\big\}H_{A}^{f}(q,k_{\perp})

As we will see in Sect.
8
, cancellation of terms
\sim g^{\parallel}_{\mu\nu}
proportional to
h_{A}
in the r.h.s of this equation is
actually a consequence of (EM) gauge invariance.

Let us now assemble the contribution of terms (
86
) to
W_{\mu\nu}
. Summing Eqs. (
95
), (
103
), and (
121
) we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptV_{\mu\nu}(q)~=~{1\over 32\pi^{4}}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\ast}d^{2}x_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}-i\beta x_{\ast}+i(q,x)_{\perp}}

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt\big[\langle A,B|({\bar{\psi}}_{A}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x))({\bar{\psi}}_{B}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Xi_{1}^{n}(0))+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle+x\leftrightarrow 0\big]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{g_{\mu\nu}^{\parallel}\over\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}sN_{c}}\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}\Big\{(k,q-k)_{\perp}F^{f}(q,k_{\perp})-~{1\over m^{2}}k_{\perp}^{2}(q-k)_{\perp}^{2}H^{f}(q,k_{\perp})\Big\}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~{1\over\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}sN_{c}}\!\int d^{2}k_{\perp}{1\over m^{2}}\big\{[k^{\perp}_{\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu](k,q-k)_{\perp}-k_{\perp}^{2}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt-~(q-k_{\perp})^{2}k^{\perp}_{\mu}k^{\perp}_{\nu}+g_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}(k,q-k)_{\perp}^{2}-g_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}k_{\perp}^{2}(q-k_{\perp})^{2}\big]\big\}H_{A}^{f}(q,k_{\perp})

(122)

Finally, to get
W_{\mu\nu}^{(2a)}(q)
of Eq. (
42
) we need to add
the contribution of the term
[{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{B}(x)\big]\big[{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{A}(0)\big]
.
Similarly to the case of one quark-quark-gluon operator considered in Sect.
6.1
, it can be demonstrated that this contribution doubles the result (
122
) so we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptW_{\mu\nu}^{(2a)}(q)~=~{1\over 32\pi^{4}}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\ast}d^{2}x_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}-i\beta x_{\ast}+i(q,x)_{\perp}}\big\{\langle A,B|[{\bar{\psi}}_{A}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x)]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\times[{\bar{\psi}}_{B}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Xi_{1}^{n}(0)]+[{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{B}(x)\big]\big[{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{A}(0)\big]+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle+x\leftrightarrow 0\big\}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{2g_{\mu\nu}^{\parallel}\over Q_{\parallel}^{2}N_{c}}\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}\Big\{(k,q-k)_{\perp}F^{f}(q,k_{\perp})-~{1\over m^{2}}k_{\perp}^{2}(q-k)_{\perp}^{2}H^{f}(q,k_{\perp})\Big\}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+~{2\over Q_{\parallel}^{2}N_{c}}\!\int d^{2}k_{\perp}{1\over m^{2}}\big\{[k^{\perp}_{\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu](k,q-k)_{\perp}-k_{\perp}^{2}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt-~(q-k_{\perp})^{2}k^{\perp}_{\mu}k^{\perp}_{\nu}+g_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}(k,q-k)_{\perp}^{2}-g_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}k_{\perp}^{2}(q-k_{\perp})^{2}\big]\big\}H_{A}^{f}(q,k_{\perp})

(123)

where
Q_{\parallel}^{2}\equiv\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}s

6.3
Term with two quark-quark-gluon operators coming from
{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}
and
\Xi_{2}

Let us start with the first term in Eq. (
43
).

\hskip 0.0pt\check{W}^{(2b)}_{1\mu\nu}~=~{N_{c}\over s}\langle A,B|\big[\bar{\psi}_{A}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Xi_{2}(x)\big]\big[{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{A}(0)\big]+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(124)

After Fierz transformation (
237
) we obtain

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{W}^{(2b)}_{1\mu\nu}~=~-{N_{c}\over 2s}(\delta_{\mu}^{\alpha}\delta_{\nu}^{\beta}+\delta_{\nu}^{\alpha}\delta_{\mu}^{\beta}-g_{\mu\nu}g^{\alpha\beta})\langle A,B|\big\{[\bar{\psi}_{A}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\alpha}\psi_{A}^{n}(0)][{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\beta}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x)]

(125)

\displaystyle\hskip 156.49014pt+~\gamma_{\alpha}\otimes\gamma_{\beta}\leftrightarrow\gamma_{\alpha}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{\beta}\gamma_{5}\big\}|A,B\rangle

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~{N_{c}\over 2s}(\delta_{\mu}^{\alpha}\delta_{\nu}^{\beta}+\delta_{\nu}^{\alpha}\delta_{\mu}^{\beta}-{1\over 2}g_{\mu\nu}g^{\alpha\beta})\langle A,B|[\bar{\psi}_{A}^{m}(x)\sigma_{\alpha\xi}\psi_{A}^{n}(0)][{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}^{n}(0)\sigma_{\beta}^{~\xi}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x)]|A,B\rangle~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(note that
{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}\Xi_{2}={\bar{\Xi}}_{2}\gamma_{5}\Xi_{2}=0
).
Using explicit expressions (
26
) for quark fields and separating color-singlet terms we get

\check{W}^{(2b)}_{1\mu\nu}~=~\check{V}^{4}_{\mu\nu}+\check{V}^{5}_{\mu\nu}

(126)

where

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{V}^{4}_{\mu\nu}~=~-{1\over s^{3}}(\delta_{\mu}^{\alpha}p_{1\nu}+\delta_{\nu}^{\alpha}p_{1\mu}-g_{\mu\nu}p_{1}^{\alpha})\Big(\langle\bar{\psi}(x)A_{j}(x)\gamma_{\alpha}A_{i}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\times~\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\gamma^{i}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\Big)~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(127)

and

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{V}^{5}_{\mu\nu}=~{1\over s^{3}}(\delta_{\mu}^{\alpha}\delta_{\nu}^{\beta}+\delta_{\nu}^{\alpha}\delta_{\mu}^{\beta}-{1\over 2}g_{\mu\nu}g^{\alpha\beta})

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\times~\Big\{-p_{1\beta}\langle\bar{\psi}(x)A_{j}(x)\sigma_{\alpha k}A_{i}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\gamma^{i}\sigma_{\bullet}^{~k}\gamma^{j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\langle\bar{\psi}(x)A_{j}(x)\sigma_{\alpha{\bullet}}A_{i}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\gamma^{i}\sigma_{{\bullet}\beta_{\perp}}\gamma^{j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}\Big\}~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(128)

We will consider them in turn.

6.3.1
Term proportional to
f_{1}{\bar{f}}_{1}

Let us start with
g_{\mu\nu}
term in Eq. (
127
).

\hskip 0.0pt{g_{\mu\nu}\over s^{3}}\langle\bar{\psi}(x)A_{j}(x){\not\!p}_{1}A_{i}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\gamma^{i}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+{\not\!p}_{1}\otimes\gamma_{j}\leftrightarrow{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{j}\gamma_{5}

(129)

It is obvious that the target matrix element can bring factor
s
. On the contrary, as we discussed above, the projectile
matrix element cannot produce
s
since

\langle\bar{\psi}(x)A_{j}(x)\gamma_{\alpha}A_{i}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}~\sim~{p_{2\alpha}\over p_{1}\cdot p_{2}}\times[g_{ij}\phi(x_{\perp}^{2})+x_{i}x_{j}\xi(x_{\perp}^{2})]~+~...

(130)

Indeed, since projectile matrix elements know about
p_{2}
only through the direction of Wilson lines,
the l.h.s. can be proportional only to factor
{p_{2\alpha}\over p_{1}\cdot p_{2}}
that does not change under rescaling of
p_{2}
.
Also, due to Eq. (
267
)
\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\otimes{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}
can be replaced by

-{1\over\beta_{q}^{2}}\langle\bar{\psi}(0)\otimes\psi(x)\rangle_{B}
.
Consequently, the r.h.s. of Eq. (
129
) is
\sim g_{\mu\nu}{m_{\perp}^{4}\over\beta_{q}^{2}s^{2}}

\hskip 0.0pt{g_{\mu\nu}\over s^{3}}\langle\bar{\psi}(x)A_{j}(x){\not\!p}_{1}A_{i}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\gamma^{i}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+{\not\!p}_{1}\otimes\gamma_{j}\leftrightarrow{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{j}\gamma_{5}~\sim~g_{\mu\nu}{m_{\perp}^{4}\over\beta_{q}^{2}s^{2}}

(131)

which is
O\big({m_{\perp}^{2}\alpha_{q}\over\beta_{q}s}\big)
in comparison to Eq. (
123
).

We get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{V}^{4}_{\mu\nu}~=~-{p_{1\mu}\over s^{3}}\Big(\langle\bar{\psi}(x)A_{j}(x)\gamma_{\nu}A_{i}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\gamma^{i}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\Big)~+~\mu\leftrightarrow\nu~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(132)

If the index
\nu
is transverse, the contribution of this equation to
W_{\mu\nu}
is of order of

\check{V}^{4}_{\mu\nu}~\sim~p_{1\mu}q^{\perp}_{\nu}{m_{\perp}^{2}\over\beta_{q}^{2}s^{2}}

(133)

which is
O\big({m_{\perp}^{2}\over\beta_{q}s}\big)
in comparison to Eq. (
85
).

For the longitudinal indices
\mu
and
\nu
we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{V}^{4}_{\mu\nu}~=~-{4p_{1\mu}p_{1\nu}\over s^{4}}\Big(\langle\bar{\psi}(x)A_{j}(x){\not\!p}_{2}A_{i}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\gamma^{i}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~{g^{\parallel}_{\mu\nu}\over s^{3}}\Big(\langle\bar{\psi}(x)A_{j}(x){\not\!p}_{1}A_{i}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\gamma^{i}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\Big)~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(134)

Similarly to Eq. (
131
), the contribution of the second term to
W_{\mu\nu}
is

\sim{g^{\parallel}_{\mu\nu}m_{\perp}^{4}\over\beta_{q}^{2}s^{2}}~=~O\Big({\alpha_{q}m_{\perp}^{2}\over\beta_{q}s}\Big)\times\big[{\rm r.h.s.~of~Eq.~(\ref{w2atvet})}\big]

(135)

so we are left with the first term in the r.h.s. of Eq. (
134
). Using Eq. (
244
)
it can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{V}^{4}_{\mu\nu}~=~-{4p_{1\mu}p_{1\nu}\over s^{4}}\Big(\langle\bar{\psi}(x){\not\!\!A}(x){\not\!p}_{2}{\not\!\!A}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0){\not\!p}_{1}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\Big)~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(136)

The corresponding contribution to
W_{\mu\nu}
is obtained from QCD equation of motion (
280
) and formula (
267
) from
Appendix
11.3
:

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptV^{4}_{\mu\nu}(q)~=~{4p_{1\mu}p_{1\nu}\over\beta_{q}^{2}s^{2}N_{c}}\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}k_{\perp}^{2}F^{f}(q,k_{\perp})

(137)

6.3.2
Term proportional to
h_{1}^{\perp}{\bar{h}}_{1}^{\perp}

Let us start with
g_{\mu\nu}
term in Eq. (
128
).

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{g_{\mu\nu}\over s^{3}}\langle\bar{\psi}(x)A_{j}(x)\sigma_{{\bullet}k}A_{i}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\gamma^{i}\sigma_{\bullet}^{~k}\gamma^{j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(138)

The target matrix element is proportional to
s
while the projectile one cannot bring
s
due to Eq. (
130
), so the contribution
of the r.h.s of Eq. (
138
) to
W_{\mu\nu}
is of order

\displaystyle\sim~{g_{\mu\nu}\over\beta_{q}^{2}s^{2}}m_{\perp}^{4}~=~O\big({m_{\perp}^{2}\alpha_{q}\over s\beta_{q}}\Big)\times\big[{\rm r.h.s.~of~Eq.~(\ref{w2atvet})}\big]

(139)

similarly to Eq. (
135
). We get

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91692pt\check{V}^{5}_{\mu\nu}=~{1\over s^{3}}\Big\{-p_{1\mu}\langle\bar{\psi}(x)A_{j}(x)\sigma_{\nu k}A_{i}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\gamma^{i}\sigma_{\bullet}^{~k}\gamma^{j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91692pt+~\langle\bar{\psi}(x)A_{j}(x)\sigma_{\mu{\bullet}}A_{i}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\gamma^{i}\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}\gamma^{j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}\Big\}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(140)

Let us at first consider the second term in this formula:

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91692pt{1\over s^{3}}\langle\bar{\psi}(x)A_{j}(x)\sigma_{\mu_{\perp}{\bullet}}A_{i}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\gamma^{i}\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}\gamma^{j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91692pt+~{2p_{1\mu}\over s^{4}}\langle\bar{\psi}(x)A_{j}(x)\sigma_{\ast{\bullet}}A_{i}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\gamma^{i}\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}\gamma^{j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}\Big\}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu

(141)

Similarly to Eq. (
130
), projectile matrix elements cannot give factor
s
so the corresponding contribution to
W_{\mu\nu}
is
of order of

(aq^{\perp}_{\mu}q^{\perp}_{\nu}+bq_{\perp}^{2}g^{\perp}_{\mu\nu}){m_{\perp}^{2}\over\beta_{q}^{2}s^{2}}~~~~{\rm or}~~~{2p_{1\mu}\over s^{3}}{m_{\perp}^{4}\over\beta_{q}^{2}s^{2}}

(142)

that are
O\big({\alpha_{q}m_{\perp}^{2}\over\beta_{q}s}\big)
in comparison to Eqs. (
85
) and (
123
), respectively.

We are left with

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{V}^{5}_{\mu\nu}=~-{p_{1\mu}\over s^{3}}\langle\bar{\psi}(x)A_{j}(x)\sigma_{\nu k}A_{i}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\gamma^{i}\sigma_{\bullet}^{~k}\gamma^{j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu+x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-{4p_{1\mu}p_{1\nu}\over s^{4}}\langle\bar{\psi}(x)A_{j}(x)\sigma_{\ast k}A_{i}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\gamma^{i}\sigma_{\bullet}^{~k}\gamma^{j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~{g^{\parallel}_{\mu\nu}\over s^{4}}\langle\bar{\psi}(x)A_{j}(x)\sigma_{{\bullet}k}A_{i}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\gamma^{i}\sigma_{\bullet}^{~k}\gamma^{j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~\Big({p_{1\mu}\over s^{3}}\langle\bar{\psi}(x)A_{j}(x)\sigma_{\nu k}A_{i}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\gamma^{i}\sigma_{\bullet}^{~k}\gamma^{j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\Big)+x\leftrightarrow 0

(143)

First, note that the two last terms are small, of order of Eq. (
142
), for the same reason as Eq. (
140
) above. As to
the first term in r.h.s. of Eq. (
143
), using Eq. (
243
) it can be rewritten as

\hskip 0.0pt\check{V}^{5}_{\mu\nu}~=~-{4p_{1\mu}p_{1\nu}\over s^{4}}\langle\bar{\psi}(x){\not\!\!A}(x)\sigma_{\ast k}{\not\!\!A}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\sigma_{\bullet}^{~k}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(144)

so the corresponding contribution to
W_{\mu\nu}
takes the form

\hskip 0.0ptV^{5}_{\mu\nu}~=~-{4p_{1\mu}p_{1\nu}\over\beta_{q}^{2}s^{2}N_{c}}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}{1\over m^{2}}k_{\perp}^{2}(k,q-k)_{\perp}H^{f}(q,k_{\perp})

(145)

where we used Eqs. (
267
) and (
281
).

The full result for
W_{\mu\nu}^{(2b)}
is given by the sum of Eqs. (
137
) and (
145
)

\hskip 0.0ptW_{1\mu\nu}^{(2b)}~=~{4p_{1\mu}p_{1\nu}\over\beta_{q}^{2}s^{2}N_{c}}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}\Big[k_{\perp}^{2}F^{f}(q,k_{\perp})-~{1\over m^{2}}k_{\perp}^{2}(k,q-k)_{\perp}H^{f}(q,k_{\perp})\Big]

(146)

6.3.3
Second term in Eq. (
43
)

Let us start now consider the second term in Eq. (
43
).

\hskip 0.0pt\check{W}^{(2b)}_{2\mu\nu}~=~{N_{c}\over s}\langle A,B|\big[{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{B}(x)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Xi_{1}(0)\big]+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(147)

After Fierz transformation (
237
) we obtain

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{W}^{(2b)}_{2\mu\nu}~=~-{N_{c}\over 2s}(\delta_{\mu}^{\alpha}\delta_{\nu}^{\beta}+\delta_{\nu}^{\alpha}\delta_{\mu}^{\beta}-g_{\mu\nu}g^{\alpha\beta})\langle A,B|\big\{[{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\alpha}\Xi_{1}^{n}(0)][{\bar{\psi}}_{B}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\beta}\psi_{B}^{m}(x)]

(148)

\displaystyle\hskip 156.49014pt+~\gamma_{\alpha}\otimes\gamma_{\beta}\leftrightarrow\gamma_{\alpha}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{\beta}\gamma_{5}\big\}|A,B\rangle

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~{N_{c}\over 2s}(\delta_{\mu}^{\alpha}\delta_{\nu}^{\beta}+\delta_{\nu}^{\alpha}\delta_{\mu}^{\beta}-{1\over 2}g_{\mu\nu}g^{\alpha\beta})\langle A,B|[{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\sigma_{\alpha\xi}\Xi_{1}^{n}(0)][\psi_{1B}^{n}(0)\sigma_{\beta}^{~\xi}\psi_{1B}^{m}(x)]|A,B\rangle~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

Sorting out color-singlet terms, we get similarly to sum of Eqs. (
127
) and (
128
)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{W}^{(2b)}_{2\mu\nu}~=~-{1\over s^{3}}(\delta_{\mu}^{\alpha}p_{2\nu}+\delta_{\nu}^{\alpha}p_{2\mu}-g_{\mu\nu}p_{2}^{\alpha})\Big(\langle\bar{\psi}(x)B_{j}(x)\gamma_{\alpha}B_{i}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\times~\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\alpha}\big)(0)\gamma^{i}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}+\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\Big)~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~{1\over s^{3}}(\delta_{\mu}^{\alpha}\delta_{\nu}^{\beta}+\delta_{\nu}^{\alpha}\delta_{\mu}^{\beta}-{1\over 2}g_{\mu\nu}g^{\alpha\beta})

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\times~\Big\{-p_{2\alpha}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\alpha}\big)(0)\gamma^{i}\sigma_{\ast}^{~k}\gamma^{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}(x)B_{j}(x)\sigma_{\beta k}B_{i}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\alpha}\big)(0)\gamma^{i}\sigma_{\ast\alpha_{\perp}}\gamma^{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}(x)B_{j}(x)\sigma_{\beta\ast}B_{i}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{B}\Big\}~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(149)

Starting from this point, all calculations repeat those of Sections
6.3.1
and
6.3.2
with replacements of

p_{1}\leftrightarrow p_{2}
,
\alpha_{q}\leftrightarrow\beta_{q}
and exchange of projectile matrix elements and the target ones. The result is
Eq. (
150
) with these replacements so we finally get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptW_{\mu\nu}^{(2b)}~=~{4p_{1\mu}p_{1\nu}\over\beta_{q}^{2}s^{2}N_{c}}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}\Big[k_{\perp}^{2}\big[f_{1}^{f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})F^{f}(q,k_{\perp})-~{1\over m^{2}}k_{\perp}^{2}(k,q-k)_{\perp}H^{f}(q,k_{\perp})\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~{4p_{2\mu}p_{2\nu}\over\alpha_{q}^{2}s^{2}N_{c}}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}\Big[(q-k)_{\perp}^{2}F^{f}(q,k_{\perp})-~{1\over m^{2}}(q-k)_{\perp}^{2}(k,q-k)_{\perp}H^{f}(q,k_{\perp})\Big]

(150)

6.4
Third term with two quark-quark-gluon operators

Let us consider the first term in the r.h.s. of Eq. (
44
). After Fierz transformation it turns to

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 2}[({\bar{\Xi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x))(\bar{\psi}_{B}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{A}^{n}(0))+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-{g_{\mu\nu}\over 4}({\bar{\Xi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\psi_{A}^{n}(0))(\bar{\psi}_{B}^{n}(0)\Xi_{2}^{m}(x))+{g_{\mu\nu}\over 4}({\bar{\Xi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\gamma_{5}\psi_{A}^{n}(0))(\bar{\psi}_{B}^{n}(0)\gamma_{5}\Xi_{2}(x))

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~{g_{\mu\nu}\over 4}({\bar{\Xi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\alpha}\psi_{A}^{n}(0))(\bar{\psi}_{B}(0)\gamma^{\alpha}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x))+{g_{\mu\nu}\over 4}({\bar{\Xi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\alpha}\gamma_{5}\psi_{A}(0))(\bar{\psi}_{B}^{n}(0)\gamma^{\alpha}\gamma_{5}\Xi_{2}(x))

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~{1\over 4}[({\bar{\Xi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{A}^{n}(0))(\bar{\psi}_{B}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x))+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu]

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt-~{1\over 4}({\bar{\Xi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\psi_{A}^{n}(0))(\bar{\psi}_{B}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x))+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~{1\over 4}[({\bar{\Xi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\sigma_{\nu\alpha}\psi_{A}^{n}(0))(\bar{\psi}_{B}^{n}(0)\sigma_{\mu\alpha}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x))+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu]

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt-~{g_{\mu\nu}\over 8}({\bar{\Xi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\sigma^{\alpha\beta}\psi_{A}^{n}(0))(\bar{\psi}_{B}^{n}(0)\sigma_{\alpha\beta}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x))

(151)

Let us demonstrate that after sorting out color-singlet matrix elements the contribution
W_{\mu\nu}^{(2c)}
is
O\big({1\over N_{c}^{2}}\big)
in comparison to

W_{\mu\nu}^{(2a)}
(and
W_{\mu\nu}^{(2b)}
). Consider a typical term in the r.h.s. of Eq. (
151
)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{N_{c}\over s}\langle A,B|{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\Gamma_{1}\psi_{A}^{n}(0))(\bar{\psi}_{B}^{n}(0)\Gamma_{2}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x))|A,B\rangle

(152)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{N_{c}\over s}\langle A,B|\big(\bar{\psi}_{A}^{k}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)A_{j}^{ml}(x)\gamma^{i}{\not{p}_{2}\over s}\Gamma_{1}\psi^{n}_{A}(0))(\bar{\psi}_{B}^{n}(0)\Gamma_{2}{\not{p}_{1}\over s}\gamma^{j}B^{km}_{i}(x){1\over\beta}\psi_{B}^{l}(x))|A,B\rangle

After separation of color singlet contributions

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\langle A,B|({\bar{\psi}}_{A}^{k}(A_{i})^{ml}\psi_{A}^{n})({\bar{\psi}}_{B}^{n}(B_{j})^{km}\psi_{B}^{l})|A,B\rangle

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~\langle{\bar{\psi}}_{A}^{k}(A_{i})^{km}\psi_{A}^{n}\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}_{B}^{n}(B_{j})^{ml}\psi_{B}^{l})\rangle_{B}-~if^{abc}\langle{\bar{\psi}}_{A}^{k}t^{c}_{kl}A_{i}^{a}\psi_{A}^{n}\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}_{B}^{n}B_{j}^{b}\psi_{B}^{l}\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{1\over N_{c}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}_{A}A_{i}\psi_{A}\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}_{B}B_{j}\psi_{B}\rangle_{B}-2if^{abc}\langle A^{a}_{i}({\bar{\psi}}_{A}t^{c}t^{d}\psi_{A})\rangle_{A}\langle B^{b}_{j}({\bar{\psi}}_{B}t^{d}\psi_{B})\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 125.19212pt-~{2\over N_{c}}f^{abc}\langle A^{a}_{i}({\bar{\psi}}_{A}t^{c}\psi_{A})\rangle_{A}\langle B^{b}_{j}({\bar{\psi}}_{B}\psi_{B})\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{1\over N_{c}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}_{A}A_{i}\psi_{A}\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}_{B}B_{j}\psi_{B}\rangle_{B}-2i{f^{abc}\over N_{c}^{2}-1}\langle A^{a}_{i}({\bar{\psi}}_{A}t^{c}t^{b}\psi_{A})\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}_{B}B_{j}\psi_{B}\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-{1\over N_{c}(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\langle{\bar{\psi}}_{A}A_{i}\psi_{A}\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}_{B}B_{j}\psi_{B}\rangle_{B}

(153)

we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{N_{c}\over s}\langle A,B|{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\Gamma_{1}\psi_{A}^{n}(0))(\bar{\psi}_{B}^{n}(0)\Gamma_{2}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x))|A,B\rangle

(154)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-{1\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)s^{3}}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}_{A}{1\over\alpha}\big)A_{j}(x)\gamma^{i}\not{p}_{2}\Gamma_{1}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}(0)\Gamma_{2}\not{p}_{1}\gamma^{j}B_{i}(0){1\over\beta}\psi_{B}(x))\rangle_{B}

Since projectile and target matrix elements can bring
s
each and
{1\over\alpha}
and
{1\over\beta}
convert to

{1\over\alpha_{q}}
and
{1\over\beta_{q}}
, the typical contribution of (
154
) to
W^{\mu\nu}(q)
is

\sim~{1\over N_{c}^{2}}\times\bigg(g_{\perp}^{\mu\nu}{q_{\perp}^{2}\over\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}s},~~{q_{\perp}^{\mu}q_{\perp}^{\nu}\over\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}s},~~g_{\parallel}^{\mu\nu}{q_{\perp}^{2}\over\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}s}\bigg)

(155)

In Ref.
[
28
]
we calculated the sum of these structures
corresponding to convolution of
\mu
and
\nu
. In principle, one can repeat that calculation and find contribution to
these structures separately. However, since the corresponding matrix elements of quark-quark-gluon operators are
virtually unknown, in this paper we we will disregard such
{1\over N_{c}^{2}}
terms.

Thus, the contribution of Eq. (
34
) to
W_{\mu\nu}(q)
is given in the leading order in
N_{c}
by the sum of equations
(
85
), (
123
), and (
150
).

7
Power corrections from
J_{A}^{\mu}(x)J_{B}^{\nu}(0)
terms

Power corrections of the second type come from the terms

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Psi_{1}(x){\bar{\Psi}}_{2}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Psi_{2}(0)~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(156)

where
\Psi_{1}
and
\Psi_{2}
are given by Eq. (
26
).

12
12

12

In the appendix 8.3.2 to
[
28
]
it is demonstrated that higher-order terms in the expansion Eq. (
25
)
(denoted by dots) are small in our kinematical region
s\gg Q^{2}\gg q_{\perp}^{2}
.
We get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\big[\big(\bar{\psi}_{A}+{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}\big)(x)\gamma_{\mu}\big(\psi_{A}+\Xi_{1}\big)(x)\big][\big(\bar{\psi}_{B}+{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}\big)(0)\gamma_{\nu}\big(\psi_{B}+\Xi_{2}\big)(0)\big]~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~[\bar{\psi}_{A}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{A}(x)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{B}(0)\big]

(157)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~[{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{A}(x)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{B}(0)\big]+[\bar{\psi}_{A}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Xi_{1}(x)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{B}(0)\big]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~[\bar{\psi}_{A}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{A}(x)\big]\big[{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{B}(0)\big]+[\bar{\psi}_{A}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{A}(x)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Xi_{2}(0)\big]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~[{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Xi_{1}(x)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{B}(0)\big]+[\bar{\psi}_{A}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{A}(x)\big]\big[{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Xi_{2}(0)\big]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~[{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{A}(x)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Xi_{2}(0)\big]+[\bar{\psi}_{A}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Xi_{1}(x)\big]\big[{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{B}(0)\big]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~[{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{A}(x)\big]\big[{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{B}(0)\big]+[\bar{\psi}_{A}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Xi_{1}(x)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Xi_{2}(0)\big]~+~x\leftrightarrow 0.

First, let us demonstrate that contributions to
W_{\mu\nu}
from the second to fifth lines in eq. (
157
) vanish.
Obviously, matrix element of the operator in the second line vanishes. Formally,

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\int\!dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}}\langle p_{A}|{\hat{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\gamma_{\mu}{\hat{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})|p_{A}\rangle~=~\delta(\alpha_{q})\langle p_{A}|{\hat{\psi}}(0)\gamma_{\mu}{\hat{\psi}}(0)|p_{A}\rangle,

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\int\!dx_{\ast}~e^{-i\beta_{q}x_{\ast}}\langle p_{B}|{\hat{\psi}}(0)\gamma_{\nu}{\hat{\psi}}(0)|p_{B}\rangle~=~\delta(\beta_{q})\langle p_{B}|{\hat{\psi}}(0)\gamma_{\nu}{\hat{\psi}}(0)|p_{B}\rangle

(158)

and, non-formally, one hadron cannot produce the DY pair on its own.

It is easy to see that contributions to
\check{W}_{\mu\nu}
from the third and the fourth lines in Eq. (
157
) vanish
due to the absence of color-singlet structure.
Indeed, let us consider for example the term

\hskip 0.0pt[{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{A}(x)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{B}(0)\big]~=~-\big[\big(\bar{\psi}_{A}^{m}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\gamma^{i}B^{mn}_{i}{\not{p}_{2}\over s}\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{A}^{n}(x)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}^{l}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{B}^{l}(0)\big]

(159)

The corresponding term in
\check{W}_{\mu\nu}
is

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-{N_{c}\over s}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}^{m}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\gamma^{i}{\not{p}_{2}\over s}\gamma_{\mu}\psi^{n}(x)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}^{l}(0)B^{mn}_{i}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi^{l}(0)\rangle_{B}~+~\mu\leftrightarrow\nu

(160)

which obviously does not have color-singlet contribution. Similarly, other three terms in the third and fourth lines in Eq. (
157
) vanish.

Next, let us demonstrate that the contribution of the fifth line in Eq. (
157
) vanishes for the same
reason as in Eq. (
158
). Let is consider for example the first term in the fifth line

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt[{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Xi_{1}(x)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{B}(0)\big]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{2p_{2\mu}\over s^{2}}\big[\big(\bar{\psi}_{A}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\gamma^{i}B_{i}(x)\not{p}_{2}\gamma^{j}B_{j}(x){1\over\alpha}\psi_{A}(x)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{B}(0)\big]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{p_{2\mu}\over N_{c}s^{2}}\big[\big(\bar{\psi}_{A}^{m}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\gamma^{i}\not{p}_{2}\gamma^{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi_{A}^{m}(x)\big]\big[B^{a}_{i}B^{a}_{j}(x)\bar{\psi}^{n}_{B}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi^{n}_{B}(0)\big]

(161)

where we separated color-singlet contribution in the last line. The corresponding term in
W_{\mu\nu}
is

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 64\pi^{6}N_{c}}{2p_{2\mu}\over s^{3}}\!\int d^{2}k_{\perp}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}d^{2}x_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\!\int\!dx_{\ast}d^{2}x^{\prime}_{\perp}e^{-i\beta x_{\bullet}+i(q-k,x^{\prime})_{\perp}}

(162)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\times~\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\gamma^{i}\not{p}_{2}\gamma^{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}\langle B^{a}_{i}(x_{\ast},x^{\prime}_{\perp})B^{a}_{j}(x_{\ast},x^{\prime}_{\perp})\bar{\psi}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~\delta(\alpha_{q}){1\over 32\pi^{5}N_{c}}{2p_{2\mu}\over s^{3}}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\alpha}\big)(0)\gamma^{i}\not{p}_{2}\gamma^{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt\times~\!\int\!dx_{\ast}d^{2}x^{\prime}_{\perp}e^{-i\beta x_{\bullet}+i(q,x^{\prime})_{\perp}}\langle B^{a}_{i}(x_{\ast},x^{\prime}_{\perp})B^{a}_{j}(x_{\ast},x^{\prime}_{\perp})\bar{\psi}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}~=~0

Similarly, the contribution of the second term in the fifth line of Eq. (
157
) will be proportional to
\delta(\beta_{q})
and hence
vanish.

Let us now discuss the non-vanishing contributions coming from last two lines in Eq. (
157
).
For example, the first term in the sixth line is

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt[{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{A}(x)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Xi_{2}(0)\big]~=~\big(\bar{\psi}_{A}^{m}{1\over\alpha}\big)\gamma^{i}B^{mn}_{i}{\not{p}_{2}\over s}\gamma_{\mu}\psi^{n}_{A}(x)\bar{\psi}^{k}_{B}\gamma_{\nu}{\not{p}_{1}\over s}\gamma^{j}A^{kl}_{j}{1\over\beta}\psi_{B}^{l}(0)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~\big[\big(\bar{\psi}_{A}^{m}{1\over\alpha}\big)\gamma^{i}A^{kl}_{j}(0){\not{p}_{2}\over s}\gamma_{\mu}\psi^{n}_{A}(x)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}^{k}_{B}\gamma_{\nu}{\not{p}_{1}\over s}\gamma^{j}B^{mn}_{i}(x){1\over\beta}\psi_{B}^{l}(0)\big]

(163)

Separating color-singlet contributions with the help of the formula

\langle{\bar{\psi}}_{m}A^{a}_{i}\psi_{n}\rangle~=~{2t^{a}_{mn}\over N_{c}^{2}-1}\langle{\bar{\psi}}A_{i}\psi\rangle

(164)

we get the corresponding term in
\check{W}_{\mu\nu}
in the form

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{N_{c}\over N_{c}^{2}-1}{2\over s^{3}}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)A_{j}(0)\gamma^{i}\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{\mu}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\not{p}_{1}\gamma^{j}B_{i}(x){1\over\beta}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}

(165)

which is similar to Eq. (
96
) with exception of extra color factor
{N_{c}\over N_{c}^{2}-1}\simeq{1\over N_{c}}
.
Consequently, as discussed in Sect.
6.2.2
, non-negligible contributions come from transverse
\mu

and
\nu
only. We calculate them in next Section.

7.1
Last two lines in eq. (
157
)

In this section we calculate the traceless part of sixth and seventh lines Eq. (
157
). Since we consider only transverse

\mu
and
\nu
, to simplify notations we will call them
m
and
n
in this Section.
Using eq. (
26
) and separating color-singlet matrix elements with the help of Eq. (
164
), we
rewrite the traceless part of sixth and seventh lines in Eq. (
157
) as

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 2}\big([{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}(x)\gamma_{m}\psi_{A}(x)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}(0)\gamma_{n}\Xi_{2}(0)\big]+[\bar{\psi}_{A}(x)\gamma_{n}\Xi_{1}(x)\big]\big[{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}(0)\gamma_{n}\psi_{A}(0)\big]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+[{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}(x)\gamma_{m}\psi_{A}(x)\big]\big[{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}(0)\gamma_{n}\psi_{B}(0)\big]+[\bar{\psi}_{A}(x)\gamma_{m}\Xi_{1}(x)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}(0)\gamma_{n}\Xi_{2}(0)\big]+m\leftrightarrow n\big)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{1\over 2(N_{c}^{2}-1)s^{2}}\Big(\big[\big({\bar{\psi}}_{A}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\gamma^{j}\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{m}A_{k}(0)\psi_{A}(x)\big]\big[{\bar{\psi}}_{B}(0)\gamma_{n}\not{p}_{1}\gamma^{k}B_{j}(x){1\over\beta}\psi_{B}(0)\big]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\big[{\bar{\psi}}_{A}(x)\gamma_{n}\not{p}_{2}\gamma^{j}A_{k}(0){1\over\alpha}\psi_{A}(x)\big]\big[\big({\bar{\psi}}_{B}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\gamma^{k}\not{p}_{1}\gamma_{m}B_{j}(x)\psi_{B}(0)\big]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\big[\big({\bar{\psi}}_{A}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\gamma^{j}\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{m}A_{k}(0)\psi_{A}(x)\big]\big[\big({\bar{\psi}}_{B}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\gamma^{k}\not{p}_{1}\gamma_{n}B_{j}(x)\psi_{B}(0)\big]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\big[{\bar{\psi}}_{A}(x)\gamma_{m}\not{p}_{2}\gamma^{j}A_{k}(0){1\over\alpha}\psi_{A}(x)\big]\big[{\bar{\psi}}_{B}(0)\gamma_{n}\not{p}_{1}\gamma^{k}B_{j}(x){1\over\beta}\psi_{B}(0)\big]+m\leftrightarrow n\Big)

(166)

To save space, hereafter we do not display subtraction of trace with respect to
m,n
indices but it is always assumed.
Using formulas (
258
) we can write down the contribution to
\check{W}_{\mu\nu}
from sixth and seventh lines in Eq. (
157
) in the form

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{W}^{\rm 6+7th}_{mn}=~{N_{c}\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)s^{3}}\Big(\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{m}(0)\psi(x)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}(0)\breve{B}_{n}(x)\not{p}_{1}{1\over\beta}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+\langle\bar{\psi}(x)\breve{A}_{m}(0)\not{p}_{2}{1\over\alpha}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{n}(x)\psi(0)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{m}(0)\psi(x)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{n}(x)\psi(0)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\langle\bar{\psi}(x)\breve{A}_{m}(0)\not{p}_{2}\big({1\over\alpha}\psi\big)(x)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}(0)\breve{B}_{n}\not{p}_{1}\big({1\over\beta}\psi\big)(0)\rangle_{B}+m\leftrightarrow n\Big)~+~x\leftrightarrow 0,

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{N_{c}\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)s^{3}}\Big(\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{m}(0)\psi(x)+\bar{\psi}(x)\breve{A}_{m}(0)\not{p}_{2}{1\over\alpha}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\times~\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{n}(x)\psi(0)\rangle_{B}+\bar{\psi}(0)\breve{B}_{n}(x)\not{p}_{1}{1\over\beta}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}+m\leftrightarrow n\Big)~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(167)

Let us now consider corresponding matrix elements. It is easy to see that

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\breve{A}_{i}(0)\not{p}_{2}{1\over\alpha}\psi(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{1\over\alpha}{1\over 4\pi^{3}s^{2}}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\!\int_{-\infty}^{x_{\bullet}}\!dx^{\prime}_{\bullet}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{p}_{2}[F_{\ast i}+i\gamma_{5}{\tilde{F}}_{\ast i}](0)\psi(x^{\prime}_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt=~{k_{i}\over\alpha}j_{1}(\alpha,k_{\perp}),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{i}(0)\psi(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-{1\over\alpha}{1\over 4\pi^{3}s^{2}}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\!\int_{-\infty}^{x_{\bullet}}\!dx^{\prime}_{\bullet}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x^{\prime}_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{p}_{2}[F_{\ast i}-i\gamma_{5}{\tilde{F}}_{\ast i}](0)\psi(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A},

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt=~-{k_{i}\over\alpha}{\bar{j}}_{1}(\alpha,k_{\perp}),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)\breve{A}_{i}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{p}_{2}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-{1\over\alpha}{1\over 4\pi^{3}s^{2}}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\!\int_{-\infty}^{0}\!dx^{\prime}_{\bullet}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)\not{p}_{2}[F_{\ast i}+i\gamma_{5}{\tilde{F}}_{\ast i}](x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\psi(x^{\prime}_{\bullet},0_{\perp})\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt=~-{k_{i}\over\alpha}{\bar{j}}^{\star}_{1}(\alpha,k_{\perp}),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(0)\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{i}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\psi(0)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{1\over\alpha}{1\over 4\pi^{3}s^{2}}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\!\int_{-\infty}^{x_{\bullet}}\!dx^{\prime}_{\bullet}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x^{\prime}_{\bullet},0_{\perp})\not{p}_{2}[F_{\ast i}-i\gamma_{5}{\tilde{F}}_{\ast i}](x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\psi(0)\rangle_{A},

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt=~{k_{i}\over\alpha}j_{1}^{\star}(\alpha,k_{\perp}),

(168)

where we used parametrization (
285
). For the target matrix elements, we obtain

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\ast}~e^{-i\beta x_{\ast}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\breve{B}_{i}(0)\not{p}_{1}{1\over\beta}\psi(x_{\ast},x_{\perp})\rangle_{B}~=~{k_{i}\over\beta}j_{1}(\beta,k_{\perp}),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\ast}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\ast}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\beta}\big)(x_{\ast},x_{\perp})\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{i}(0)\psi(x_{\ast},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}~=~-{k_{i}\over\beta}{\bar{j}}_{1}(\beta,k_{\perp}),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\ast}~e^{-i\beta x_{\ast}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)\breve{B}_{i}(x_{\ast},x_{\perp})\not{p}_{1}{1\over\beta}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}~=~-{k_{i}\over\beta}{\bar{j}}^{\star}_{1}(\beta,k_{\perp}),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\ast}~e^{-i\beta x_{\ast}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{i}(x_{\ast},x_{\perp})\psi(0)\rangle_{A}~=~{k_{i}\over\beta}j_{1}^{\star}(\beta,k_{\perp}),

(169)

The corresponding contribution to (traceless)

W(\alpha_{q},\beta_{q},x_{\perp})
takes the form

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptW_{mn}^{\rm 6+7th}(q)-{\rm trace}~=~{s/2\over(2\pi)^{4}N_{c}}\int\!d^{4}x~e^{-iqx}\big(\check{W}_{mn}^{\rm 6+7th}(x_{\perp})-{\rm trace}\big),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{1\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}s}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}[(j_{1}-{\bar{j}}_{1})(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})(j^{\star}_{1}-{\bar{j}}^{\star}_{1})(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})+{\rm c.c.}]

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt\times~[k_{m}(q-k)_{n}+m\leftrightarrow n+g_{mn}(k,q-k)_{\perp}]

(170)

where we have recovered the subtraction of trace.

The trace part can be obtained in a similar way. Using Eq. (
257
) one gets

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptg^{mn}\check{W}^{\rm 6+7th}_{mn}=~{2N_{c}\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)s^{3}}\Big(\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\breve{A}_{m}(0)\not{p}_{2}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}(0)\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}^{m}(x){1\over\beta}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+\langle\bar{\psi}(x)\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{m}(0){1\over\alpha}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\breve{B}^{m}(x)\not{p}_{1}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\breve{A}_{m}(0)\not{p}_{2}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\breve{B}^{m}(x)\not{p}_{1}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\langle\bar{\psi}(x)\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{m}(0)\big({1\over\alpha}\psi\big)(x)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}(0)\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}^{m}\big({1\over\beta}\psi\big)(0)\rangle_{B}+m\leftrightarrow n\Big)~+~x\leftrightarrow 0,

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{2N_{c}\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)s^{3}}\Big(\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\breve{A}_{m}(0)\not{p}_{2}\psi(x)+\bar{\psi}(x)\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{m}(0){1\over\alpha}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\times~\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\breve{B}^{m}(x)\not{p}_{1}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}+\bar{\psi}(0)\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}^{m}(x){1\over\beta}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}\Big)~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(171)

The corresponding contribution to trace part of

W(\alpha_{q},\beta_{q},x_{\perp})
takes the form

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptg^{mn}W_{mn}^{\rm 6+7th}(q)~=~{s/2\over(2\pi)^{4}N_{c}}\int\!d^{4}x~e^{-iqx}g^{mn}\check{W}_{mn}^{\rm 6+7th}(x_{\perp})

(172)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-{2\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}s}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}(k,q-k)_{\perp}[(j_{2}-{\bar{j}}_{2})(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})(j^{\star}_{2}-{\bar{j}}^{\star}_{2})(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})+{\rm c.c.}]

which agrees with Eq. (6.2) from Ref.
[
28
]
after
replacements
j_{2}=j_{2}^{\rm tw3}-i{\tilde{j}}_{2}^{\rm tw3}
and
{\bar{j}}_{2}=j_{1}^{\rm tw3}+i{\tilde{j}}_{1}^{\rm tw3}
.
It should be noted that the difference between
j_{1}
and
j_{2}
in traceless
vs
trace part is due to difference in formulas
(
258
) and (
257
).

Thus, the result is the sum of Eqs. (
170
) and
(
172
)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptW_{\mu\nu}^{\rm 6+7th}(q)~=~{s/2\over(2\pi)^{4}N_{c}}\int\!d^{4}x~e^{-iqx}\check{W}_{mn}^{\rm 6+7th}(x_{\perp})

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{1\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}s}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}\Big([(j_{1}-{\bar{j}}_{1})(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})(j^{\star}_{1}-{\bar{j}}^{\star}_{1})(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})+{\rm c.c.}]

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt\times~[k_{\mu}(q-k)_{\nu}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu+g^{\perp}_{\mu\nu}(k,q-k)_{\perp}]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~g^{\perp}_{\mu\nu}(k,q-k)_{\perp}[(j_{2}-{\bar{j}}_{2})(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})(j^{\star}_{2}-{\bar{j}}^{\star}_{2})(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})+{\rm c.c.}]

(173)

As we mentioned in the Introduction, in this paper we we will take into account only leading and sub-leading terms in
N_{c}

and leave the
{1\over N_{c}^{2}}
corrections discussed above for future publications.

Finally, as proved in Appendix
11.5
, we can neglect contributions proportional to the product of quark and gluon TMDs.

8
Results and estimates

8.1
Results

Assembling Eqs. (
38
), (
85
), (
123
), (
150
), and (
173
) we get
the result for
W_{\mu\nu}(q)
that consists of two parts:

W_{\mu\nu}(q)~=~W^{1}_{\mu\nu}(q)+W^{2}_{\mu\nu}(q)

(174)

The first, gauge-invariant, part is given by

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptW^{1}_{\mu\nu}(q)~=~W^{1F}_{\mu\nu}(q)+W^{1H}_{\mu\nu}(q),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptW^{1F}_{\mu\nu}(q)~=~\sum_{f}e_{f}^{2}W^{fF}_{\mu\nu}(q),~~~~W^{fF}_{\mu\nu}(q)~=~{1\over N_{c}}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}F^{f}(q,k_{\perp}){\cal W}^{F}_{\mu\nu}(q,k_{\perp}),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptW^{1H}_{\mu\nu}(q)~=~\sum_{f}e_{f}^{2}W^{fH}_{\mu\nu}(q),~~~~W^{fH}_{\mu\nu}(q)~=~{1\over N_{c}}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}H^{f}(q,k_{\perp}){\cal W}^{H}_{\mu\nu}(q,k_{\perp})

(175)

where
F^{f}
and
H^{f}
are given by Eq. (
68
) and

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{\cal W}^{F}_{\mu\nu}(q,k_{\perp})~=~-g_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}+{1\over Q_{\parallel}^{2}}(q^{\parallel}_{\mu}q^{\perp}_{\nu}+q^{\parallel}_{\nu}q^{\perp}_{\mu})+{q_{\perp}^{2}\over Q_{\parallel}^{4}}q^{\parallel}_{\mu}q^{\parallel}_{\nu}+{{\tilde{q}}_{\mu}{\tilde{q}}_{\nu}\over Q_{\parallel}^{2}}[q_{\perp}^{2}-4(k,q-k)_{\perp}]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt-~\Big[{{\tilde{q}}_{\mu}\over Q_{\parallel}^{2}}\Big(g^{\perp}_{\nu i}-{q^{\parallel}_{\nu}q_{i}\over Q_{\parallel}^{2}}\Big)(q-2k)_{\perp}^{i}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\Big]

(176)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptm^{2}{\cal W}_{\mu\nu}^{H}(q,k_{\perp})~

(177)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-k^{\perp}_{\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}-k^{\perp}_{\nu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\mu}-g_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}(k,q-k)_{\perp}+2{{\tilde{q}}_{\mu}{\tilde{q}}_{\nu}-q^{\parallel}_{\mu}q^{\parallel}_{\nu}\over Q_{\parallel}^{4}}k_{\perp}^{2}(q-k)_{\perp}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~\Big({q^{\parallel}_{\mu}\over Q_{\parallel}^{2}}\big[k_{\perp}^{2}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}+k^{\perp}_{\nu}(q-k)_{\perp}^{2}\big]+~{{\tilde{q}}_{\mu}\over Q_{\parallel}^{2}}\big[k_{\perp}^{2}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}-k^{\perp}_{\nu}(q-k)_{\perp}^{2}\big]+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~{{\tilde{q}}_{\mu}{\tilde{q}}_{\nu}+q^{\parallel}_{\mu}q^{\parallel}_{\nu}\over Q_{\parallel}^{4}}\big[q_{\perp}^{2}-2(k,q-k)_{\perp}\big](k,q-k)_{\perp}-~{q^{\parallel}_{\mu}{\tilde{q}}_{\nu}+{\tilde{q}}_{\mu}q^{\parallel}_{\nu}\over Q_{\parallel}^{4}}(2k-q,q)_{\perp}(k,q-k)_{\perp}

where
q^{\parallel}_{\mu}\equiv\alpha_{q}p_{1}+\beta_{q}p_{2}
and
{\tilde{q}}_{\mu}\equiv\alpha_{q}p_{1}-\beta_{q}p_{2}
. These are the same expressions as in Eq. (
6
) if
one identifies
x_{A}
with
\alpha_{q}
and
x_{B}
with
\beta_{q}
and neglects
O\big({m^{2}\over s}\big)
terms
in
p_{A}
and
p_{B}
and
O\big({q_{\perp}^{4}\over Q^{4}}\big)
corrections due to difference between
Q^{2}
and
Q_{\parallel}^{2}
.
It is easy to see that
q^{\mu}W^{F}_{\mu\nu}~=~0
and
q^{\mu}W^{H}_{\mu\nu}~=~0
. Note that

q^{\mu}W^{F}_{\mu\nu}
and
q^{\mu}W^{H}_{\mu\nu}
are exactly zero without any
{q_{\perp}^{2}\over Q^{2}}
corrections.
This is similar to usual “forward” DIS, but different from off-forward DVCS where the cancellations of right-hand sides of Ward identities
involve infinite towers of twists
[
42
,
43
,
44
]

The second part is

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptW^{2}_{\mu\nu}(q)~=~{1\over N_{c}}\sum_{f}e_{f}^{2}{1\over Q^{2}}\!\int d^{2}k_{\perp}\bigg[{1\over m^{2}}\big\{[k^{\perp}_{\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu](k,q-k)_{\perp}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~k_{\perp}^{2}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}-(q-k_{\perp})^{2}k^{\perp}_{\mu}k^{\perp}_{\nu}+g_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}(k,q-k)_{\perp}^{2}-g_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}k_{\perp}^{2}(q-k_{\perp})^{2}\big]\big\}H_{A}^{f}(q,k_{\perp})

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~{N_{c}\over N_{c}^{2}-1}\Big([k^{\perp}_{\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu+g^{\perp}_{\mu\nu}(k,q-k)_{\perp}]J_{1}^{f}(q,k_{\perp})

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt-~g^{\perp}_{\mu\nu}(k,q-k)_{\perp}J_{2}^{f}(q,k_{\perp})\Big)~+~O\big({1\over N_{c}^{2}}\big)\bigg]~+~O\big({Q_{\perp}^{4}\over Q^{4}}\big)

(178)

where
H_{A}
is given by Eq. (
110
) and

\displaystyle\hskip-31.29802ptJ_{1}^{f}(q,k_{\perp})~=~(j_{1}-{\bar{j}}_{1})(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})(j^{\star}_{1}-{\bar{j}}^{\star}_{1})(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})+{\rm c.c.}

\displaystyle\hskip-31.29802ptJ_{2}^{f}(q,k_{\perp})~=~(j_{2}-{\bar{j}}_{2})(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})(j^{\star}_{2}-{\bar{j}}^{\star}_{2})(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})+{\rm c.c.}

(179)

These terms are not gauge invariant:
q^{\mu}W^{2}_{\mu\nu}(q)\neq 0
. The reason is that gauge invariance is restored after adding terms like
{m_{\perp}^{2}\over Q^{2}}\times{\rm Eq.(\ref{pc})}
which we do not calculate in this paper. Indeed, for example,

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptq^{\mu}W^{2}_{\mu\nu}(q)~\sim~{q^{\perp}_{\nu}q_{\perp}^{2}\over\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}s}~~~~{\rm and}~~~q^{\mu}\times~{p_{2}^{\mu}q_{\perp}^{\nu}q_{\perp}^{2}\over\alpha_{q}^{2}\beta_{q}s^{2}}~=~{q^{\perp}_{\nu}q_{\perp}^{2}\over\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}s}

(180)

They are of the same order so one should expect that gauge invariance is restored after calculation of the terms
\sim{p_{2}^{\mu}q_{\perp}^{\nu}q_{\perp}^{2}\over\alpha_{q}^{2}\beta_{q}s^{2}}
which are beyond the scope of this paper.
For the same reason we see that all structures in Eq. (
39
) except

{g_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}q_{\perp}^{2}\over\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}s}

and
{q^{\perp}_{\mu}q^{\perp}_{\nu}\over\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}s}
are determined by leading-twist TMDs
f_{1}
and
h_{1}^{\perp}
.

Sometimes it is convenient to represent hadronic tensor in transverse coordinate space. Introducing

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\begin{array}[]{c}f(\alpha,b_{\perp})\\
{\bar{f}}(\alpha,b_{\perp})\end{array}\bigg\}~=~\int\!{d^{2}k_{\perp}\over 4\pi^{2}}~e^{i(k,b)_{\perp}}\bigg\{\begin{array}[]{c}f(\alpha,k_{\perp})\\
{\bar{f}}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\end{array}

(and similarly for target TMDs) we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptW^{1F}_{\mu\nu}(\alpha_{q},\beta_{q},b_{\perp})~

(186)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~4\pi^{2}\sum_{f}e_{f}^{2}\Big\{-g_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}+{i\over Q_{\parallel}^{2}}(q^{\parallel}_{\mu}\partial^{\perp}_{\nu}+q^{\parallel}_{\nu}\partial^{\perp}_{\mu})-{q^{\parallel}_{\mu}q^{\parallel}_{\nu}+{\tilde{q}}_{\mu}{\tilde{q}}_{\nu}\over Q_{\parallel}^{2}}\partial_{\perp}^{2}\Big]f_{1}{\bar{f}}_{1}-~{4{\tilde{q}}_{\mu}{\tilde{q}}_{\nu}\over Q_{\parallel}^{2}}(\partial_{i}f_{1}^{f})(\partial^{i}{\bar{f}}_{1}^{f})

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt-~\Big[{{\tilde{q}}_{\mu}\over Q_{\parallel}^{2}}\Big(\delta_{\nu}^{i}-{q^{\parallel}_{\nu}\over Q_{\parallel}^{2}}i\partial^{i}\Big)(f_{1}\partial_{i}{\bar{f}}_{1}^{f}-{\bar{f}}_{1}\partial_{i}f_{1}^{f})+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\Big]+f_{1}\leftrightarrow{\bar{f}}_{1}\Big\}+O(\alpha_{s})

where

f_{1}=f_{1}(\alpha_{q},b_{\perp})
,
{\bar{f}}_{1}\equiv{\bar{f}}_{1}(\beta_{q},b_{\perp})
everywhere except

f\leftrightarrow{\bar{f}}
terms where it is opposite (the question about rapidity cutoffs for TMDs will be addressed in Sect.

9
).

Similarly, we can write down
W^{H}
contribution in coordinate space. For future use, however, it is convenient to define Fourier transform in a slightly different way. Introduce
h_{i}(k_{\perp})\equiv k_{i}h_{1}^{\perp}(k),~{\bar{h}}_{i}(k_{\perp})\equiv k_{i}{\bar{h}}_{1}^{\perp}(k)
and

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\begin{array}[]{c}h_{i}(\alpha,b_{\perp})\\
